All you do is upload a single design file and choose which products to apply it to. The system automatically scales and previews it for you.\n\n## Key Customization Features\n\nThe designer tool offers several core customization capabilities:\n\n**Design Manipulation**: Click on the design to display the customization block and available colors to the right of the designer. Rotate the design and adjust its orientation. Adjust the size of the design and adapt it to the t-shirt or item chosen. By moving the white circle away or closer to the center of the design, you can adjust the size of the design. The white circle is in the lower right corner of the frame that surrounds the design.\n\n**Customer Personalization**: Buyers can customise certain products by adding their own text, adjusting the colours, or repositioning elements on the garment.\n\n**Text and Upload Options**: Enabling the uploading feature adds the uploading feature to your Shop's designer. Allow Customers to Add Text adds the text customization tool to your Shop's designer. Customers will be able to add text to products in all sorts of colors and fonts.\n\n## Print Area Guidelines\n\nIn the Design Tool, you can see a square that's either black or white. That's the print area. Printful's fonts ensure high-quality results for all fulfillment techniques, but if you have your own font, you can use it too.\n\n### Image Upload\nCreate your design in any image editor, upload the file to the Design Maker, and tweak it to your liking.\n\n### Clipart and Design Elements\nStart with a blank canvas or enhance your design with over 20,496 clipart graphics organized by themes. Quick Designs are right for you if you want to combine clipart with eye-catching messages—they're pre-made compositions of text and clipart.\n\n### Canvas and Print Area Management\nMake sure to keep your design within the safe print area to avoid your design being cut off during printing. Navigate through different views to ensure your artwork covers every side of the garment—front, back, and sleeves.\n\n### Pattern Tool\nUse the Pattern Tool to create repeat patterns with clipart, uploads, or sample files. Reimagining the online shopping experience. | nclud - A Provocative Creative Agency\",\"url\":\"https://nclud.com/work/customink/\"},{\"title\":\"How To Re-Size Your Custom Ink Clipart In The Design Lab\",\"url\":\"https://www.customink.com/help_center/size-your-clipart\"},{\"title\":\"Custom T-shirts: Design & Make Your Own Custom Shirts Online\",\"url\":\"https://www.customink.com/products/t-shirts/4\"},{\"title\":\"How to Create a Web-to-Print E-store Like CustomInk\",\"url\":\"https://www.printxpand.com/blog/create-web-to-print-estore-like-customink/\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's a comprehensive step-by-step overview of how CustomInk Design Lab works:\n\n## Step-by-Step Process\n\n### 1. **Select Your Product**\nStart by selecting the item you want to customize. Custom Ink offers a wide variety: t-shirts, sweatshirts, hats, jackets, bags, water bottles, and more. Filter products based on color, style, fabric, brand, price, or gender/youth/unisex fit.\n\n### 2. **Design Your Product**\nOnce you click \"Start Designing\", your chosen product will load in our design lab where you can upload artwork, add text, and preview your design. The Design Lab offers several tools:\n\n- **Add Text**: Pick fonts, styles, and sizes.\n- **Upload or Choose Graphics**: Upload your logo or artwork, or choose from thousands of built-in graphics.\n- **Position and Resize**: Position and resize each design element using drag-and-drop controls.\n- **Adjust Colors**: Use this button to change the color of the current product.\n\n### 3. **Preview Your Design**\nPreview your item in real time to see exactly how it will look.\n\n### 4. **Additional Design Features**\nAdd additional product styles or additional product colors to your design. Zoom in to show your design in more detail. Integrate the T-Shirt Designer into Your Shop.\",\"url\":\"https://www.spreadshop.com/blog/2016/12/02/its-here-integrate-the-t-shirt-designer-into-your-shop/\"},{\"title\":\"Spreadshirt T-Shirt Design Templates - Placeit\",\"url\":\"https://placeit.net/make-money-with-spreadshirt/\"},{\"title\":\"Help with the Customize Tool – Spreadshirt\",\"url\":\"https://help.spreadshirt.com/hc/en-us/articles/360000052513-Help-with-the-Customize-Tool\"},{\"title\":\"Spreadshirt Design Guide: Tips and Tricks for Creating Eye-Catching Merchandise\",\"url\":\"https://www.topbubbleindex.com/blog/spreadshirt-design-guide/\"},{\"title\":\"How to customize a t-shirt online: how to use it\",\"url\":\"https://www.design-your-t-shirt.com/how-to-customize-my-t-shirt.html\"},{\"title\":\"Custom T-shirts With Your Individual Design | Spreadshirt\",\"url\":\"https://www.spreadshirt.com/custom/t-shirts\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's information about the Spreadshirt t-shirt designer tool and its features:\n\n## Designer Tool Overview\n\nSpreadshirt's t-shirt designer allows you to choose from almost 50 different T-shirt types in a variety of colors and styles.\n\n## Key Features\n\n**Canvas/Print Area:**\nSpreadshirt has a built-in warning system that alerts you if any part of your design extends beyond the designated print area, requiring adjustment of size or placement to ensure it falls within the printable boundaries.\n\n**Color Picker:**\nYou can modify the design color or colors by clicking on the design to display the customization block and available colors.\n\n**Text Upload:**\nThe text customization tool allows customers to add text to products in all sorts of colors and fonts.\n\n**Image Upload:**\nYou can upload your own image or photo, making sure the image is big enough and has a high enough resolution so it will look good on your T-shirt. Supported file formats are PNG, JPG, BMP or GIF with a maximum file size of 10 MB, an image resolution of at least 200 dpi, and a maximum size of 4000 x 4000 pixels.\n\n**Design Customization:**\nYou can effortlessly adjust the size, alignment, and even rotate your artwork to achieve the desired visual effect.\n\n## Checkout and Ordering\n\nBefore you order, you will get a complete breakdown of the overall costs, and there is no minimum order size - you can order a single custom T-shirt or 1,000 custom T-shirts.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"a3a0ac39-11a8-4123-a4a2-ff8e32cdcb0f","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:41.191Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9d865d0f-7357-4935-9b3b-ae1b06a5ff54"}

Here's what I found:\n\n## Canva T-Shirt Design & Mockup Workflow\n\nBefore using Canva's t-shirt mockup generator, finalize your design using the editor, then select one of their mockups and upload your design to start.\n\n### Design Canvas & Specifications\nOpen a new canvas (recommended: 4500x5400 px at 300 DPI), choose a t-shirt template or start from scratch, and add fonts, graphics, or upload your own images.\n\n### Garment Mockup & Visualization\nCanva has a tool called Mockups that shows you how your design will look on a t-shirt, and it even lets you see the design on different models, including women, men, toddlers and infants, making it much easier to visualize your design before you send it to print. A brilliant feature of Mockups is that it's a live mockup so as you're changing the design, you can see how it will affect the final design on the t-shirt.\n\n### Print Area & Design Placement\nWhen you create t-shirt mockups, carefully position and scale your shirt design to give it a consistent look, and you can conveniently adjust the size, orientation, and position to make it seamless in its placement.\n\n### AI-Powered Mockup Generation\nWith the power of AI, Mockups can analyze your images and identify ideal surfaces for product placement, so that you can turn any branded photo into a custom mockup template in seconds.\n\n### Printing & Production\nLook for the \"Print t-shirt\" button in the top right corner next to the share button; once you're satisfied with your design, choose the quantity of t-shirts you want to print, and select the size and style of the t-shirt you want to use, then click \"Continue\" and follow the prompts to complete your order.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"a219d8b4-9040-40f6-adc6-60c128f97be2","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:42.325Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"87159c43-b593-44a7-a6f3-a4fc7d556cff"}

– Printful Help Center\",\"url\":\"https://help.printful.com/hc/en-us/articles/4918634599836-What-is-the-Custom-Mockup-Maker-and-how-to-use-it\"},{\"title\":\"Free online Design Maker for custom product designs | Printful\",\"url\":\"https://www.printful.com/design-maker\"},{\"title\":\"Mastering Printful Mockup Generator: Design Tips & Tricks\",\"url\":\"https://www.topbubbleindex.com/blog/printful-mockup-generator/\"},{\"title\":\"Design Maker Tutorial | Printful\",\"url\":\"https://www.printful.com/academy/lessons/design-maker-tutorial\"},{\"title\":\"Custom Products—Design Your Own or Sell Online | Printful\",\"url\":\"https://www.printful.com/design-your-own-products\"},{\"title\":\"Design Tips & Tools / Mockups – Printful Help Center\",\"url\":\"https://help.printful.com/hc/en-us/categories/360002555680-Design-Tips-Tools-Mockups\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's comprehensive information about Printful Design Maker features:\n\n## Key Features\n\n**Text Tool:** The built-in Text Tool lets you create meaningful messages and eye-catching designs in minutes. The fonts ensure high-quality results for all fulfillment techniques, but you can also use your own font.\n\n**Pattern Tool:** The Pattern Tool turns any element into a bold pattern that meets your creative standards. Unlike the duplication tool, the pattern tool places duplicated items in a new layer so you can adjust each layer's size, color, and alignment separately.\n\n**Clipart Library:** The Design Maker has over 50 clipart collections and nearly 3000 individual graphics—all free to use, including minimalistic designs, timeless classics, and design elements for special occasions.\n\n**Mockup Generator:** The Mockup Generator is a powerful tool that allows you to create life-like images of your designs, and you can preview your design right inside the Design Maker. You can design any Printful product and choose from 2,550+ mockups to showcase it, putting apparel on diverse models, placing products in different scenes, or picking a classic white background.\n\n**Design Customization:** You can customize your designs with background colors and patterns, and download mockups to see how they would look in real life.\n\n**Design Steps:** The tool lets you create stunning print-on-demand designs in just a few steps.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"6424bc43-91b9-4f07-8c66-19498ded2fe0","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:43:19.775Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9dd3fce-4f61-4b21-a7f0-c042156d6778"}

Here's what I found based on the search results:\n\n## CustomInk Design Lab Features\n\nThe Design Lab allows users to upload a logo, add text, or choose from thousands of clipart and font options to create their look.\n\n**Key Tools and Features:**\n\n- **Upload Tool**: The Upload button allows you to upload your own artwork into the Design Lab.\n\n- **Clipart Library**: Users can add pieces of clipart using the \"Add Art\" tab on the top left-hand side of the Lab, where they can search or browse for clipart and click it to add it to their design. {"parentUuid":"19973d39-43df-4aad-b648-724d5c1f5908","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a132d24f7d8d0e78f","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017UuHxffNnhF7mLNCt4wLjZ","type":"tool_result","content":"Web search results for query: \"print on demand design editor comparison Printful CustomInk Canva Spreadshirt features pros cons 2025 2026\"\n\nLinks: [{\"title\":\"My 11 BEST Print on Demand Companies & Sites (March 2026)\",\"url\":\"https://www.michaelessek.com/print-on-demand-companies/\"},{\"title\":\"T-shirt design software: The 12 best tools in 2026 | Printful\",\"url\":\"https://www.printful.com/blog/t-shirt-design-software\"},{\"title\":\"Printful vs Spreadshirt: Which is Better for Print on Demand? - Bootstrapping Ecommerce\",\"url\":\"https://bootstrappingecommerce.com/printful-vs-spreadshirt/\"},{\"title\":\"Top 15 Spreadshirt Alternatives And Competitors In 2025\",\"url\":\"https://www.gelato.com/blog/spreadshirt-alternatives\"},{\"title\":\"Custom Ink vs. Printful vs. Spreadshirt Comparison\",\"url\":\"https://sourceforge.net/software/compare/Custom-Ink-vs-Printful-vs-Spreadshirt/\"},{\"title\":\"10 Printify Alternatives for Print On Demand 2026 | Podbase\",\"url\":\"https://www.podbase.com/blogs/printify-alternatives\"},{\"title\":\"Top 10 Best Print on Demand Platforms to Sell Better in 2025\",\"url\":\"https://trueprofit.io/blog/print-on-demand-platforms\"},{\"title\":\"Top 13 Custom Ink Alternatives for Print-on-Demand in 2026\",\"url\":\"https://fourthwall.com/blog/custom-ink-alternatives-for-print-on-demand\"},{\"title\":\"Printful vs. Spreadshirt: Print-on-demand comparison | Printful\",\"url\":\"https://www.printful.com/printful-vs-spod\"},{\"title\":\"Canva vs Printful - 2026 Comparison - Software Advice\",\"url\":\"https://www.softwareadvice.com/graphic-design/canva-profile/vs/printful/\"}]\n\nBased on the web search results, here's a comprehensive comparison of these print-on-demand design editors:\n\n## Design Tools & Features\n\nTools like Printful's Design Maker, Canva, and Kittl offer drag-and-drop simplicity and pre-made templates ideal for beginners. Printful seems to have the better design tools overall, allowing you to access free resources (like clipart) and adjust your designs in dozens of different ways. You also get a handy AI tool that helps you upscale images to boost product quality.\n\nCanva is a popular design tool used for just about every type of graphic you can think of. Its drag-and-drop interface makes it easy to design custom t-shirts no matter how much experience you have. Canva offers a free version with basic features as well as several paid plans for individuals, business teams, and educational institutions. Pro: $12.99 per month or $119.99 paid annually.\n\nAdd to that a beginner-friendly design editor, 250+ product options, and fulfillment in 18 countries, and you've got a platform that makes selling custom shirts both professional and accessible. (This refers to Spreadshirt)\n\n## Product Selection & Pricing\n\nPrintful's huge range has 480 customizable products, including apparel products, accessories, and Home & Living items like pillows, candles, and yoga mats. Spreadshirt's catalog is closer to 350, mostly t-shirts, hoodies, and accessories.\n\nCustom Ink has been around long enough to be a household name in custom shirt printing. It's straightforward, US-focused, and gives teams and individuals an easy way to put logos on shirts, mugs, tote bags, and other custom products.\n\n## Quality & Integration\n\nIn terms of product quality, Printful definitely has a better reputation. Although I definitely recommend ordering samples of products from any POD service, Printful constantly earns better reviews for it's luxurious garment materials, and crisp, clean prints.\n\nPrintful connects directly to 22 eCommerce integrations, including Shopify, Etsy, and Amazon. Orders flow automatically between your store and Printful, making fulfillment incredibly easy. Spreadshirt has 4 direct integrations and no online marketplace connections. To reach more platforms, you'll need Order Desk, which adds cost and setup.\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\nPrintful is the better service overall – particularly for brands looking to scale. It gives you more products to choose from, delivers consistent quality, and makes it easy to use multiple sales channels.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"dbff450a-bebc-4454-a6b8-33974696a0b5","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:43:38.189Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19973d39-43df-4aad-b648-724d5c1f5908"}

productTitle: string\n    36→  productImage?: string\n    37→  category?: string\n    38→  productColor?: string\n    39→  onApply?: (result: { type: 'personalization' | 'composition'; id: string }) => void\n    40→  onClose?: () => void\n    41→  trigger?: React.ReactNode\n    42→}\n    43→\n    44→/** Max file size for uploaded images (5MB) */\n    45→const MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024\n    46→\n    47→export function DesignStudio({\n    48→  productId,\n    49→  productTitle,\n    50→  productImage,\n    51→  category,\n    52→  productColor,\n    53→  onApply,\n    54→  onClose,\n    55→  trigger,\n    56→}: DesignStudioProps) {\n    57→  const t = useTranslations('designStudio')\n    58→  const { authenticated } = useAuth()\n    59→\n    60→  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)\n    61→  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'text' | 'ai' | 'upload'>('text')\n    62→\n    63→  // AI Design state\n    64→  const [selectedPreset, setSelectedPreset] = useState<string | null>(null)\n    65→  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false)\n    66→  const [designUrl, setDesignUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)\n    67→  const [designOpacity, setDesignOpacity] = useState(1.0)\n    68→  const [designProvider, setDesignProvider] = useState<string | undefined>()\n    69→  const [designInferenceMs, setDesignInferenceMs] = useState<number | undefined>()\n    70→  const [generationsRemaining, setGenerationsRemaining] = useState(10)\n    71→  const [generationsTotal] = useState(10)\n    72→\n    73→  // Upload state\n    74→  const [uploadedFile, setUploadedFile] = useState<string | null>(null)\n    75→  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)\n    76→\n    77→  const handleOpenChange = useCallback(\n    78→    (isOpen: boolean) => {\n    79→      setOpen(isOpen)\n    80→      if (!isOpen) {\n    81→        onClose?.()\n    82→      }\n    83→    },\n    84→    [onClose],\n    85→  )\n    86→\n    87→  // AI Design generation handler\n    88→  const handleGenerate = useCallback(\n    89→    async (prompt: string, stylePreset?: string) => {\n    90→      if (!authenticated) return\n    91→\n    92→      setIsGenerating(true)\n    93→      try {\n    94→        const response = await fetch('/api/designs/generate', {\n    95→          method: 'POST',\n    96→          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    97→          credentials: 'include',\n    98→          body: JSON.stringify({\n    99→            productId,\n   100→            prompt,\n   101→            stylePreset: stylePreset || selectedPreset,\n   102→            category,\n   103→          }),\n   104→        })\n   105→\n   106→        if (!response.ok) {\n   107→          const errData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}))\n   108→          throw new Error(errData.error || 'Design generation failed')\n   109→        }\n   110→\n   111→        const data = await response.json()\n   112→        setDesignUrl(data.imageUrl || data.designUrl)\n   113→        setDesignProvider(data.provider)\n   114→        setDesignInferenceMs(data.inferenceMs)\n   115→        if (data.remaining !== undefined) {\n   116→          setGenerationsRemaining(data.remaining)\n   117→        }\n   118→      } catch (error) {\n   119→        console.error('Design generation error:', error)\n   120→        toast.error(\n   121→          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to generate design',\n   122→        )\n   123→      } finally {\n   124→        setIsGenerating(false)\n   125→      }\n   126→    },\n   127→    [authenticated, productId, selectedPreset, category],\n   128→  )\n   129→\n   130→  // Apply AI design\n   131→  const handleApplyAIDesign = useCallback(() => {\n   132→    if (!designUrl) return\n   133→    onApply?.({ type: 'composition', id: designUrl })\n   134→    handleOpenChange(false)\n   135→  }, [designUrl, onApply, handleOpenChange])\n   136→\n   137→  // File upload handler\n   138→  const handleFileUpload = useCallback(\n   139→    (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n   140→      const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n   141→      if (!file) return\n   142→\n   143→      if (file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE) {\n   144→        toast.error('Image must be under 5MB')\n   145→        return\n   146→      }\n   147→\n   148→      if (!['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/webp'].includes(file.type)) {\n   149→        toast.error('Only PNG, JPEG, and WebP images are supported')\n   150→        return\n   151→      }\n   152→\n   153→      const reader = new FileReader()\n   154→      reader.onload = (event) => {\n   155→        const dataUrl = event.target?.result as string\n   156→        setUploadedFile(dataUrl)\n   157→      }\n   158→      reader.readAsDataURL(file)\n   159→\n   160→      // Reset file input for re-selection\n   161→      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ''\n   162→    },\n   163→    [],\n   164→  )\n   165→\n   166→  // Apply uploaded design\n   167→  const handleApplyUpload = useCallback(() => {\n   168→    if (!uploadedFile) return\n   169→    onApply?.({ type: 'composition', id: uploadedFile })\n   170→    handleOpenChange(false)\n   171→  }, [uploadedFile, onApply, handleOpenChange])\n   172→\n   173→  // Personalization callback\n   174→  const handlePersonalized = useCallback(\n   175→    (data: PersonalizationData) => {\n   176→      onApply?.({ type: 'personalization', id: JSON.stringify(data) })\n   177→      handleOpenChange(false)\n   178→    },\n   179→    [onApply, handleOpenChange],\n   180→  )\n   181→\n   182→  // Default trigger button\n   183→  const triggerElement = trigger || (\n   184→    <Button variant=\"outline\" size=\"sm\" className=\"gap-2 min-h-[44px]\">\n   185→      <Paintbrush className=\"size-4\" />\n   186→      {t('title')}\n   187→    </Button>\n   188→  )\n   189→\n   190→  // Shared content for both Dialog and Sheet\n   191→  const studioContent = (\n   192→    <Tabs\n   193→      value={activeTab}\n   194→      onValueChange={(v) => setActiveTab(v as 'text' | 'ai' | 'upload')}\n   195→      className=\"flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden\"\n   196→    >\n   197→      <div className=\"px-4 pt-2 pb-0\">\n   198→        <TabsList className=\"w-full\">\n   199→          <TabsTrigger value=\"text\" className=\"flex-1 gap-1.5 min-h-[40px]\">\n   200→            <Type className=\"size-3.5\" />\n   201→            <span className=\"hidden sm:inline\">{t('tabText')}</span>\n   202→          </TabsTrigger>\n   203→          <TabsTrigger value=\"ai\" className=\"flex-1 gap-1.5 min-h-[40px]\">\n   204→            <Sparkles className=\"size-3.5\" />\n   205→            <span className=\"hidden sm:inline\">{t('tabAIDesign')}</span>\n   206→          </TabsTrigger>\n   207→          <TabsTrigger value=\"upload\" className=\"flex-1 gap-1.5 min-h-[40px]\">\n   208→            <Upload className=\"size-3.5\" />\n   209→            <span className=\"hidden sm:inline\">{t('tabUpload')}</span>\n   210→          </TabsTrigger>\n   211→        </TabsList>\n   212→      </div>\n   213→\n   214→      <Separator className=\"mt-2\" />\n   215→\n   216→      {/* Text tab -- wraps ProductPersonalizer */}\n   217→      <TabsContent\n   218→        value=\"text\"\n   219→        className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 m-0\"\n   220→      >\n   221→        <ProductPersonalizer\n   222→          productId={productId}\n   223→          productTitle={productTitle}\n   224→          productImage={productImage}\n   225→          category={category}\n   226→          productColor={productColor}\n   227→          onPersonalized={handlePersonalized}\n   228→        />\n   229→      </TabsContent>\n   230→\n   231→      {/* AI Design tab */}\n   232→      <TabsContent\n   233→        value=\"ai\"\n   234→        className=\"relative flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 m-0\"\n   235→      >\n   236→        {/* Auth gate overlay for unauthenticated users */}\n   237→        <AuthGateOverlay visible={!authenticated} />\n   238→\n   239→        <div className={cn('space-y-4', !authenticated && 'pointer-events-none')}>\n   240→          {/* Generation budget badge */}\n   241→          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n   242→            <p selectedPreset={selectedPreset}\n   257→            onSelect={setSelectedPreset}\n   258→          />\n   259→\n   260→          {/* Prompt editor + generate button */}\n   261→          <AIPromptEditor\n   262→            onGenerate={handleGenerate}\n   263→            isGenerating={isGenerating}\n   264→            disabled={!authenticated || generationsRemaining <= 0}\n   265→            stylePreset={selectedPreset}\n   266→          />\n   267→\n   268→          {/* AI Preview canvas */}\n   269→          {(designUrl || productImage) && (\n   270→            <>\n   271→    <Button\n   287→              onClick={handleApplyAIDesign}\n   288→              className=\"w-full min-h-[44px]\"\n   289→            >\n   290→              <Paintbrush className=\"size-4 mr-2\" />\n   291→              {t('applyDesign')}\n   292→            </Button>\n   293→          )}\n   294→        </div>\n   295→      </TabsContent>\n   296→\n   297→      {/* Upload tab */}\n   298→      <TabsContent\n   299→        value=\"upload\"\n   300→        className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 m-0\"\n   301→      >\n   302→        <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n   303→          {/* Upload area */}\n   304→          <div\n   305→            className={cn(\n   306→              'flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3',\n   307→              'rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-border',\n   308→              'p-8 text-center transition-colors',\n   309→              'hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-accent/30',\n   310→              'cursor-pointer min-h-[160px]',\n }}\n   321→          >\n   322→            <input\n   323→              ref={fileInputRef}\n   324→              type=\"file\"\n   325→              accept=\"image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp\"\n   326→  <div className=\"space-y-1\">\n   331→              <p className=\"text-sm font-medium text-foreground\">\n   332→                {t('uploadDesign')}\n   333→              </p>\n   334→              <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n   335→                {t('uploadHint')}\n   336→              </p>\n   337→            </div>\n   338→          </div>\n   339→\n   340→          {/* [\n    55→  '#000000', '#FFFFFF', '#1A1A1A', '#6B7280',\n    56→  '#DC2626', '#EA580C', '#D97706', '#CA8A04',\n    57→  '#16A34A', '#059669', '#0891B2', '#2563EB',\n    58→  '#4F46E5', '#7C3AED', '#DB2777', '#E11D48',\n    59→]\n    60→\n    61→/** Convert hex color to sRGB channel value (0-1) */\n    62→function hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {\n    63→  const cleaned = hex.replace('#', '')\n    64→  const r = parseInt(cleaned.substring(0, 2), 16) / 255\n    65→  const g = parseInt(cleaned.substring(2, 4), 16) / 255\n    66→  const b = parseInt(cleaned.substring(4, 6), 16) / 255\n    67→  return [r, g, b]\n    68→}\n    69→\n    70→/** Convert sRGB channel to linear RGB for luminance calculation */\n    71→function sRGBtoLinear(c: number): number {\n    72→  return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : Math.pow((c + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4)\n    73→}\n    74→\n    75→/** Calculate relative luminance per WCAG 2.1 */\n    76→function relativeLuminance(hex: string): number {\n    77→  const [r, g, b] = hexToRgb(hex)\n    78→  return 0.2126 * sRGBtoLinear(r) + 0.7152 * sRGBtoLinear(g) + 0.0722 * sRGBtoLinear(b)\n    79→}\n    80→\n    81→/** Calculate WCAG contrast ratio between two hex colors */\n    82→function getContrastRatio(color1: string, color2: string): number {\n    83→  const l1 = relativeLuminance(color1)\n    84→  const l2 = relativeLuminance(color2)\n    85→  const lighter = Math.max(l1, l2)\n    86→  const darker = Math.min(l1, l2)\n    87→  return (lighter + 0.05) / (darker + 0.05)\n    88→}\n    89→\n    90→/** Max file size for uploaded images (5MB) */\n    91→const MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024\n    92→\n    93→export interface PersonalizationData {\n    94→  text: string\n    95→  font: string\n    96→  fontColor: string\n    97→  fontSize: 'small' | 'medium' | 'large'\n    98→  position: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom'\n    99→  textAlign?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'\n   100→  surcharge?: number | null\n   101→}\n   102→\n 127→/**\n   128→ * Detects if text will likely overflow the printable area.\n   129→ * Uses heuristics based on font size, line count, and average character width.\n   130→ */\n   131→function detectTextOverflow(\n   132→  text: string,\n   133→  fontSize: 'small' | 'medium' | 'large',\n   134→  category?: string\n   135→): boolean {\n   136→  if (!text.trim()) return false\n   137→\n   138→  const lines = text.split('\\n')\n   139→  const printArea = getPrintArea(category)\n   140→\n   141→  // Approximate character widths per font size (in px at production print size)\n   142→  const charWidthMap = { small: 9, medium: 14, large: 21 }\n   143→  const lineHeightMap = { small: 20, medium: 30, large: 45 }\n   144→\n   145→  const charWidth = charWidthMap[fontSize]\n   146→  const lineHeight = lineHeightMap[fontSize]\n   147→\n   148→  // Calculate approximate text dimensions\n   149→  const maxLineLength = Math.max(...lines.map(l => l.length))\n   150→  const estimatedWidth = maxLineLength * charWidth\n   151→  const estimatedHeight = lines.length * lineHeight\n   152→\n   153→  // Add safety margin (10% on each side)\n   154→  const safeWidth = printArea.w * 0.8\n   155→  const safeHeight = printArea.h * 0.8\n   156→\n   157→  // Overflow if text exceeds 80% of print area (safe zone)\n   158→  return estimatedWidth > safeWidth || estimatedHeight > safeHeight\n   159→}\n   160→\n   161→/**\n   162→ * Maps product category to mockup template path.\n   163→ * Returns default if category not recognized.\n   164→ */\n   165→function getMockupTemplate(category?: string, color?: string): string {\n   166→  if (!category) return '/mockup-templates/tshirt-white.png'\n   167→\n   168→  const cat = category.toLowerCase()\n   169→  const col = color?.toLowerCase() || 'white'\n   170→\n   171→  // Map category + color to mockup template\n   172→  if (cat.includes('shirt') || cat.includes('apparel')) {\n   173→    return col.includes('black') || col.includes('dark')\n   174→      ? '/mockup-templates/hoodie-black.png'\n   180→      : '/mockup-templates/hoodie-white.png'\n   181→  }\n   182→  if (cat.includes('bag') || cat.includes('tote')) {\n   183→    return (cat.includes('phone') || cat.includes('case')) {\n   189→    return '/mockup-templates/phone-case-black.png'\n   190→  }\n   191→\n   192→  // Default fallback\n   193→  return '/mockup-templates/tshirt-white.png'\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→interface ProductPersonalizerProps {\n   197→  productId: string\n   198→  productTitle: string\n   199→  productImage?: string\n   200→ {"parentUuid":"91f11f16-e3f2-4644-9497-ee1ed13aa6cb","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a132d24f7d8d0e78f","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019FdQGnaepG3LmnPwGTGhmG","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     4→import { useLocale } from 'next-intl'\n     5→import { DESIGN_PRESETS, type DesignPreset } from '@/lib/design-presets'\n     6→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n     7→\n     8→interface StyleSelectorProps {\n     9→  selectedPreset?: string | null\n    10→  onSelect: (presetId: string | null) => void\n    11→}\n    12→\n    13→export function StyleSelector({ selectedPreset, onSelect }: StyleSelectorProps) {\n    14→  const t = useTranslations('designStudio')\n    15→  const locale = useLocale()\n    16→\n    17→  const handleSelect = (presetId: string) => {\n    18→    // Toggle selection: clicking the same preset deselects it\n    19→    if (selectedPreset === presetId) {\n    20→      onSelect(null)\n    21→    } else {\n    22→      onSelect(presetId)\n    23→    }\n    24→  }\n    25→\n    26→  return (\n    27→    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n    28→      <p className=\"text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider\">\n    29→        {t('stylePreset')}\n    30→      </p>\n    31→      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2\">\n    32→        {DESIGN_PRESETS.map((preset: DesignPreset) => {\n    33→          const isSelected = selectedPreset === preset.id\n    34→          const name = preset.name[locale] || preset.name['en']\n    35→          const description = preset.description[locale] || preset.description['en']\n    36→\n    37→          return (\n    38→            <button\n    39→              key={preset.id}\n    40→              type=\"button\"\n    41→              onClick={() => handleSelect(preset.id)}\n    42→              className={cn(\n    43→                'flex flex-col items-start gap-1 rounded-lg border p-3 text-left transition-all',\n    44→                'min-h-[44px] hover:bg-accent/50',\n    45→                isSelected\n    46→                  ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5 ring-1 ring-primary/30'\n    47→                  : 'border-border bg-card hover:border-primary/40',\n    48→              )}\n    49→            >\n    50→              {/* Color dots from suggestedColors */}\n    51→              <div className=\"flex gap-1\">\n    52→                {preset.suggestedColors.slice(0, 4).map((color) => (\n    53→                  <span\n    54→                    key={color}\n    55→                    className=\"size-2.5 rounded-full border border-border/50\"\n    56→                    style={{ backgroundColor: color }}\n    57→                  />\n    58→                ))}\n    59→              </div>\n    60→              <span className=\"text-xs font-medium text-foreground leading-tight\">\n    61→                {name}\n    62→              </span>\n    63→              <span className=\"text-[10px] text-muted-foreground leading-tight line-clamp-2\">\n    64→                {description}\n    65→              </span>\n    66→            </button>\n    67→          )\n    68→        })}\n    69→      </div>\n    70→    </div>\n    71→  )\n    72→}\n    73→"}]},"uuid":"245a2550-322c-45c2-88fb-b71ccd003645","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:43:49.224Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"91f11f16-e3f2-4644-9497-ee1ed13aa6cb"}

{"parentUuid":"03303b5b-815e-4a1a-85fe-487b7474bf83","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a132d24f7d8d0e78f","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Uu1mb1uDyGYCsdQTNccVso","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→'use client'\n     2→\n     3→import { useState } from 'react'\n     4→import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'\n     5→import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'\n     6→import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'\n     7→import { Sparkles, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'\n     8→import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'\n     9→\n    10→interface AIPromptEditorProps {\n    11→  onGenerate: (prompt: string, stylePreset?: string) => void\n    12→  isGenerating: boolean\n    13→  disabled?: boolean\n    14→  stylePreset?: string | null\n    15→}\n    16→\n    17→export function AIPromptEditor({\n    18→  onGenerate,\n    19→  isGenerating,\n    20→  disabled = false,\n    21→  stylePreset,\n    22→}: AIPromptEditorProps) {\n    23→  const t = useTranslations('designStudio')\n    24→  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('')\n    25→\n    26→  const handleGenerate = () => {\n    27→    if (!prompt.trim() || isGenerating || disabled) return\n    28→    onGenerate(prompt.trim(), stylePreset ?? undefined)\n    29→  }\n    30→\n    31→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {\n    32→    if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {\n    33→      e.preventDefault()\n    34→      handleGenerate()\n    35→    }\n    36→  }\n    37→\n    38→  return (\n    39→    <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n    40→      <Textarea\n    41→        placeholder={t('promptPlaceholder')}\n    42→        value={prompt}\n    43→        onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}\n    44→        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}\n    45→        className={cn(\n    46→          'resize-none min-h-[80px]',\n    47→          disabled && 'opacity-50',\n    48→        )}\n    49→        rows={3}\n    50→        disabled={disabled || isGenerating}\n    51→      />\n    52→      <Button\n    53→        onClick={handleGenerate}\n    54→        disabled={!prompt.trim() || isGenerating || disabled}\n    55→        className=\"w-full min-h-[44px] gap-2\"\n    56→      >\n    57→        {isGenerating ? (\n    58→          <>\n    59→            <Loader2 className=\"size-4 animate-spin\" />\n    60→            {t('generating')}\n    61→          </>\n    62→        ) : (\n    63→          <>\n    64→            <Sparkles className=\"size-4\" />\n    65→            {t('generateDesign')}\n    66→          </>\n    67→        )}\n    68→      </Button>\n    69→    </div>\n    70→  )\n    71→}\n    72→"}]},"uuid":"476d160b-a7f3-4aad-b087-f3421d4e9d16","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:43:49.877Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"03303b5b-815e-4a1a-85fe-487b7474bf83"}
{"parentUuid":"476d160b-a7f3-4aad-b087-f3421d4e9d16","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a132d24f7d8d0e78f","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01ScvbDW6xTay1Y5PE85dPad","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now I have comprehensive research on all four platforms plus a thorough understanding of our current Design Studio. Let me compile the structured comparison.\n\n---\n\n# POD Design Editor UX Research -- Comparative Analysis\n\n## 1. Platform-by-Platform Breakdown\n\n---\n\n### A. PRINTFUL DESIGN MAKER\n\n**User Flow: Product -> Color -> Size -> Design**\n\n1. User selects a product category from the catalog (480+ products).\n2. Picks a specific product (e.g., Unisex Premium T-Shirt).\n3. Lands in the Design Maker -- a full-page canvas editor opens immediately.\n4. Color and size selection happen *within* the editor toolbar, not before.\n5. The flow is: **product first, design second, color/size alongside or after**.\n\n**Garment Preview**\n\n- Uses **photorealistic mockup images** of the blank garment. NOT a flat template.\n- The garment image **changes automatically** when you switch colors (e.g., black t-shirt, navy t-shirt, etc.).\n- After design placement, you can generate **2,550+ mockups** showing the product on diverse human models, in lifestyle scenes, or on white backgrounds.\n- Front, back, and sleeve views are navigable via clickable product view thumbnails.\n\n**Design Tools Available**\n\n- **Text Tool**: Custom fonts (Printful's library), custom font upload, color selection, curved text, text effects, font grouping.\n- **Image Upload**: PNG, JPEG, SVG. Smart Image Tool auto-upscales low-DPI uploads using ML.\n- **Clipart Library**: 20,496+ free clipart graphics organized by theme (50+ collections, ~3000 individual graphics).\n- **Quick Designs**: Pre-composed text+clipart combinations ready to customize.\n- **Pattern Tool**: Turns any element into a repeating pattern. Size, color, alignment, rotation controls. Separate layer management.\n- **Layers**: Full layer management -- reorder, toggle visibility, lock. Each element is a separate layer.\n- **AI Features**: Image upscaling (Smart Image Tool, automatic), no native AI *generation* within the Design Maker itself (they recommend external tools like DALL-E, Midjourney).\n\n**Canvas vs Print Area**\n\n- Print area is shown as a **dotted/dashed rectangle** overlaid on the garment.\n- There is a \"safe zone\" indicator -- a grid icon toggle reveals the exact printable boundaries.\n- If you place elements outside the safe zone, the Design Maker warns you.\n- Canvas is the full garment view; design elements are constrained/warned within the print area.\n\n**After Design -> Cart/Checkout**\n\n- Click \"Proceed to Mockups\" to generate product images.\n- Choose mockup scenes. Then \"Add to Store\" if you are a seller, or \"Order Sample\" for direct purchase.\n- Mockup generation is a separate step between design and publishing.\n\n**Mobile Experience**\n\n- The Design Maker is **browser-based and responsive**, but Printful themselves recommend desktop for detailed work.\n- iPad support is in beta with optimized touch controls.\n- The mobile app (iOS/Android) exists but image positioning can be frustrating -- limited precision, no snapping.\n- Overall: **functional on mobile, suboptimal for complex designs**.\n\n---\n\n### B. CANVA (Custom Merch)\n\n**User Flow: Product -> Color -> Size -> Design**\n\n1. User goes to canva.com/print/custom-clothing or canva.com/t-shirts.\n2. Picks a category (T-shirts, Hoodies, Tote Bags, etc.).\n3. Chooses a template from 2000+ editable templates OR starts blank.\n4. Design happens in Canva's **universal canvas editor** (same editor as social media, presentations, etc.).\n5. Canvas dimensions for t-shirts: **4500x5400 px at 300 DPI**.\n6. After designing, user clicks \"Print T-shirt\" button in top-right corner.\n7. *Then* selects size, style, quantity, and proceeds to order.\n8. Flow: **template/blank first, design, then product options at checkout**.\n\n**Garment Preview**\n\n- During design: works on a **flat canvas** (white artboard, no garment visible).\n- To see design on garment: use the **Mockups tool** (separate from the design canvas).\n- Mockups show the design on **photorealistic garments on human models** (men, women, toddlers, infants).\n- **Live mockup**: as you edit the design, the mockup updates in real-time.\n- AI Mockup Generator can identify ideal surfaces on any photo for product placement.\n- Color switching of the garment happens within the mockup tool, but the primary design canvas is product-agnostic.\n\n**Design Tools Available**\n\n- **Text**: Thousands of fonts, text effects (shadow, outline, curve, neon), animations.\n- **Image Upload**: Any format. Background removal built-in (free).\n- **Graphics Library**: Millions of illustrations, icons, photos (some Pro-only).\n- **Magic Design (AI)**: Type a text prompt and Canva auto-generates a complete design with refined templates. Full AI generation.\n- **Magic Eraser, Magic Edit**: AI-powered editing tools.\n- **Brand Kit**: Save brand colors, fonts, logos for consistency.\n- **Elements**: Shapes, lines, frames, charts, stickers.\n- **No POD-specific tools** -- Canva is a general design platform that *happens to* print on products.\n\n**Canvas vs Print Area**\n\n- The canvas IS the full print area (4500x5400 for t-shirts). There are no garment overlays or safe zone guides within the main editor.\n- When applying to a mockup, you manually position/scale the design on the garment surface.\n- No automatic safe-zone warnings. User must manually ensure placement looks correct on the mockup.\n\n**After Design -> Cart/Checkout**\n\n- Click \"Print T-shirt\" -> select size, style, quantity -> enter shipping -> pay.\n- Supports double-sided printing (front, back, or both).\n- Free delivery included.\n\n**Mobile Experience**\n\n- **Fully featured mobile app** (iOS, Android, Windows). Same design capabilities.\n- Cross-device sync: start on mobile, continue on desktop.\n- Touch-optimized drag-and-drop.\n- **Best mobile experience of all four platforms.**\n\n---\n\n### C. CUSTOMINK DESIGN LAB\n\n**User Flow: Product -> Color -> Size -> Design**\n\n1. User browses products by category (t-shirts, sweatshirts, hats, bags, etc.). Can filter by color, style, fabric, brand, price, gender.\n2. Selects a product and clicks \"Start Designing\".\n3. The **Design Lab** loads with the chosen product displayed.\n4. Color can be changed within the lab (product color picker on the left panel).\n5. Design happens directly on the garment.\n6. After design, user enters quantities per size, then proceeds to cart/checkout.\n7. Flow: **product + color first, then design, then sizes at checkout**.\n\n**Garment Preview**\n\n- Uses **flat garment templates** (photorealistic flat-lay style), not on-model photos.\n- The garment **changes color immediately** when you pick a different color from the palette.\n- Front/back toggle via icons in the top-right corner.\n- Can design on: front, back, pocket, sleeves.\n- Zoom in/out controls to inspect detail.\n- Can add the same design to **multiple product styles** from within the lab (\"Add Products\" button).\n\n**Design Tools Available**\n\n- **Text**: Huge font selection, custom sizing, color, positioning.\n- **Upload**: Own logos/artwork (supports multiple file types). Drag-and-drop supported.\n- **Clipart Library**: 10,000+ clipart and templates organized by occasion/theme.\n- **Templates**: Hundreds of pre-designed templates, all editable.\n- **Design Services**: CustomInk offers professional design assistance if you describe your idea (unique differentiator).\n- **No AI generation** tools (as of current research).\n- **Names & Numbers feature**: For team/group orders -- assign individual names and jersey numbers per person.\n\n**Canvas vs Print Area**\n\n- The print area is implicitly defined by the garment template boundaries.\n- Design elements can be placed on specific zones (front center, left chest, back, etc.) -- each zone has its own print area.\n- Resizing/repositioning is drag-and-drop with visual feedback.\n- No explicit \"safe zone\" dotted-line indicator, but the tool constrains placement to printable areas.\n\n**After Design -> Cart/Checkout**\n\n- \"Get a Price\" or \"Add to Cart\" button.\n- Enter quantities per size.\n- Option for **group ordering**: share a link, each person picks their size and pays separately.\n- Bulk pricing displayed (price per item decreases with quantity).\n- **All-inclusive pricing** shown (no hidden fees).\n\n**Mobile Experience**\n\n- Responsive design that works on mobile browsers.\n- Touch-friendly interface with basic design capabilities.\n- Full design functionality preserved, though desktop is more comfortable for complex layouts.\n- **No dedicated mobile app** for the Design Lab specifically.\n\n---\n\n### D. SPREADSHIRT\n\n**User Flow: Product -> Color -> Size -> Design**\n\n1. User browses ~350 products, primarily apparel (50 t-shirt types in various colors/styles).\n2. Selects a product, enters the **T-Shirt Designer** / Customize Tool.\n3. Color selection available in the designer -- garment color palette on the right side.\n4. Upload a design OR add text, position it, then select size at checkout.\n5. Flow: **product first, then design + color in the editor, size at checkout**.\n\n**Garment Preview**\n\n- Shows a **flat garment illustration/template** (cleaner than a photo, more illustrated style).\n- Garment color **changes automatically** when you select a different color from the palette.\n- Print area is shown as a **visible rectangle** (black or white square depending on garment color).\n- The frame turns **green** when your design is correctly within the printable area.\n- Front/back views available.\n\n**Design Tools Available**\n\n- **Text**: Multiple fonts, colors, custom sizing.\n- **Image Upload**: PNG, JPG, BMP, GIF. Max 10MB, minimum 200 DPI, max 4000x4000 px.\n- **Design Library**: Spreadshirt's own design marketplace with pre-made designs.\n- **Resize/Rotate/Position**: Drag the white circle in the lower-right corner of the design frame to resize. Rotate and align controls.\n- **Templates**: Pre-made templates for faster product creation (seller-facing feature).\n- **No AI tools**, no clipart library comparable to Printful/CustomInk.\n\n**Canvas vs Print Area**\n\n- **Most explicit print area indicator** of all four: a visible square on the garment that is either black or white.\n- **Color-coded feedback**: frame turns green when design is within bounds.\n- Warning system alerts if design extends beyond printable boundaries.\n- This is the clearest, most beginner-friendly print area communication of the four.\n\n**After Design -> Cart/Checkout**\n\n- \"Add to Cart\" button.\n- Complete cost breakdown before ordering.\n- No minimum order (can order 1 shirt).\n- Size selection happens at checkout.\n\n**Mobile Experience**\n\n- Browser-based, works on mobile but optimized for desktop.\n- Touch controls for design positioning.\n- Upload functionality works on mobile.\n- **Basic but functional** -- no dedicated mobile app for designing.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Structured Comparison Table\n\n| Feature | Printful Design Maker | Canva (Custom Merch) | CustomInk Design Lab | Spreadshirt Designer |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **Entry Point** | Select product -> editor opens | Choose template or blank -> design -> print | Select product -> \"Start Designing\" | Select product -> customize tool |\n| **Product Selection** | 480+ products | Limited merch items (tees, hoodies, totes, mugs) | Wide catalog (tees, hats, bags, bottles) | ~350 products (apparel-focused) |\n| **Garment Preview Type** | Photorealistic mockup photos | Flat canvas (mockup tool separate) | Flat garment template (photorealistic flat-lay) | Flat garment illustration |\n| **Color Switching** | Auto-updates garment mockup | Via mockup tool (not main canvas) | Instant update in Design Lab | Instant update in designer |\n| **Front/Back Views** | Clickable view thumbnails | Via mockup tool | Toggle icons in top-right | Available |\n| **Text Tool** | Fonts + custom upload + curved text | Thousands of fonts + effects + curves + animations | Large font selection | Fonts + colors + sizing |\n| **Image Upload** | PNG/JPEG/SVG + auto-upscale | Any format + background removal | Multiple formats + drag-and-drop | PNG/JPG/BMP/GIF, 200 DPI min |\n| **Clipart / Graphics** | 20,496+ free clipart | Millions (some Pro) | 10,000+ clipart + templates | Design marketplace |\n| **AI Generation** | Image upscaling only (no generation) | Magic Design (full AI generation) | None | None |\n| **Pattern Tool** | Yes (repeat patterns, layers) | Via elements/effects | No | No |\n| **Quick/Pre-made Designs** | Quick Designs (text+clipart combos) | 2000+ templates | Hundreds of templates | Template system |\n| **Print Area Guide** | Dotted rectangle + grid toggle | None (canvas = print area) | Implicit zone boundaries | Visible rectangle + green = OK |\n| **Safe Zone Warning** | Yes (warns if outside) | No automatic warning | Constrains to printable area | Color-coded (green/red) feedback |\n| **Layer Management** | Full layers panel | Full layers panel | Basic (z-order via selection) | Basic |\n| **Mockup Generation** | 2,550+ mockup scenes + on-model photos | Mockup tool with live preview + AI surfaces | Flat garment preview only | Mockup generator available |\n| **After Design** | Proceed to Mockups -> Add to Store | Print button -> size/qty -> checkout | Get Price -> quantities per size -> cart | Add to Cart -> size -> checkout |\n| **Group/Bulk Orders** | No | No | Yes (names, numbers, group links) | No minimum orders |\n| **Design Services** | No | No | Yes (professional design help) | No |\n| **Mobile Experience** | Functional but suboptimal | Fully featured mobile app (best) | Responsive browser (decent) | Basic browser (functional) |\n| **Unique Strength** | Mockup quality + clipart depth | AI generation + universal design platform | Group ordering + design services | Clearest print area feedback |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Key Patterns Every Platform Shares\n\n1. **Product first, design second**: All four platforms start by having the user choose what they are designing FOR before opening the editor. Nobody asks you to \"design something\" without context.\n\n2. **Garment is always visible during design**: Whether photorealistic or flat-lay, the user always sees their design ON the product, not on a disconnected canvas.\n\n3. **Color switching is instant**: When available, changing the garment color updates the preview immediately. This is table-stakes UX.\n\n4. **Print area is communicated visually**: Whether dotted lines, green/red boundaries, or implicit constraints -- every tool makes clear where the design will actually print.\n\n5. **Text is the most-used tool**: Every platform puts text front and center. Upload is second. AI/clipart are power features.\n\n6. **Size is chosen LAST**: Nobody asks for size before or during design. Size is a checkout/cart concern.\n\n7. **\"Apply\" is a clear single action**: After designing, there is one clear CTA to confirm (Add to Cart, Apply, Proceed).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Gold Standard UX Flow Recommendation for SKAPARA Design Studio\n\nBased on the research, here is the recommended minimum viable flow that feels professional:\n\n### Flow Architecture\n\n```\n[Product Detail Page]\n  User sees product (variant selector for color, size)\n  Clicks \"Customize\" button\n    |\n    v\n[Design Studio Opens] -- Dialog on desktop, Sheet on mobile (we already do this)\n  |\n  +-- LEFT PANEL (or top on mobile): Live Product Preview\n  |     - Show the ACTUAL product mockup image (not a flat template)\n  |     - Overlay the user's design within a visible print area rectangle\n  |     - Print area shown as dashed border with \"safe zone\" indicator\n  |     - If user has selected a color variant, show that color's mockup\n  |     - Front/Back toggle buttons below the preview\n  |\n  +-- RIGHT PANEL (or bottom on mobile): Design Tools\n        - Tab 1: TEXT (primary -- most used)\n            - Text input (multiline, 3 lines max)\n            - Font selector (preview in font face)\n            - Color swatches + hex input\n            - Size: S/M/L buttons\n            - Position: Top/Center/Bottom\n            - Alignment: L/C/R\n        - Tab 2: AI DESIGN (differentiator for SKAPARA)\n            - Style presets grid\n            - Prompt input\n            - Generate button with cost/remaining counter\n            - Preview with opacity slider\n        - Tab 3: UPLOAD\n            - Drag-and-drop zone\n            - Background removal button\n            - Preview overlay\n```\n\n### Critical UX Requirements (What We Need to Add/Improve)\n\n**Must-Have (P0) -- These define \"professional\":**\n\n1. **Garment mockup that matches selected color**: When the user picks \"Black\" on the PDP and opens the Design Studio, they should see a black garment mockup, not a white one. Our current `getMockupTemplate()` in ProductPersonalizer has this logic but needs actual mockup template images to exist at `/mockup-templates/`. Currently these paths are referenced but the images may not exist.\n\n2. **Visible print area with safe zone**: We already have `previewZone` from `getPreviewZone(category)` -- this is good. But it should be visible by default as a subtle dashed border (not only when design content exists). Spreadshirt's approach of a persistent visible print area rectangle is best for user confidence.\n\n3. **Front/Back view toggle**: Currently absent. This is expected by users of every competitor. Even if back printing is limited, showing the back with branding placement builds trust.\n\n4. **Size selection stays on the PDP, NOT in the Design Studio**: We already do this correctly. The Design Studio should only concern itself with the design, not product configuration.\n\n**Should-Have (P1) -- Elevate above competitors:**\n\n5. **AI Design Generation (already implemented)**: Our AI tab with style presets + prompt is a genuine differentiator that Printful and CustomInk lack. This is the single biggest UX advantage SKAPARA can have over Printful. Keep and polish it.\n\n6. **Background removal on upload (already implemented)**: This matches Canva's offering. Keep it.\n\n7. **Live preview update with debounce (already implemented)**: The 500ms debounce on text preview is smart. Keep it.\n\n8. **Quick preview vs Accurate preview toggle (already implemented)**: The RadioGroup for quick/accurate mode is unique -- no competitor offers this. Polish but keep.\n\n**Nice-to-Have (P2) -- Polish features:**\n\n9. **Clipart/sticker library**: Printful has 20K+, CustomInk has 10K+. Building even a small curated set (50-100 SKAPARA-branded elements) would fill this gap.\n\n10. **Curved text**: Printful offers it, Canva offers it. Not trivial to implement but adds perceived professionalism.\n\n11. **Drag-and-drop repositioning on the canvas**: Currently we use top/center/bottom buttons. A drag handle on the preview would feel more \"design tool-like\" but the button approach is fine for MVP.\n\n12. **Design history/recent designs**: Already implemented in ProductPersonalizer. This is a feature CustomInk and Spreadshirt lack entirely.\n\n### Mobile-Specific Recommendations\n\nBased on the research, Canva is the only platform that truly excels on mobile. The others are \"functional but suboptimal.\" Our recommendation:\n\n- **Keep the Sheet component for mobile** (already implemented) -- bottom sheet is the correct pattern.\n- **90vh height is correct** -- gives enough room for preview + controls.\n- **Simplify mobile tools**: On mobile, reduce the control panels. Large touch targets (already using min-h-[44px]).\n- **Stack preview above controls** -- preview on top, scrollable controls below. Our current grid-cols-1 for mobile already does this in ProductPersonalizer.\n- **Do NOT try to build a full drag-and-drop canvas editor for mobile** -- even Printful admits this is suboptimal. Position buttons (Top/Center/Bottom) are the right mobile UX.\n\n### What Our Current Implementation Gets Right\n\nLooking at the existing code in `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/products/DesignStudio.tsx` and `/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend/src/components/products/ProductPersonalizer.tsx`:\n\n- Three-tab structure (Text / AI / Upload) -- matches industry standard of text-first with advanced options.\n- Dialog for desktop, Sheet for mobile -- correct responsive pattern.\n- Auth gating on AI features -- smart monetization/engagement pattern.\n- Generation budget display -- transparency that no competitor offers.\n- Debounced preview updates -- proper performance handling.\n- WCAG contrast checking -- accessibility awareness beyond any competitor.\n- Profanity filter -- production-ready safety feature.\n- Recent personalizations history -- reusability feature no competitor offers.\n- Print area overlay with safe zone visualization -- aligns with best practices.\n- Surcharge display -- transparent pricing.\n\n### What Needs Improvement\n\n1. **The preview garment images**: `getMockupTemplate()` references paths like `/mockup-templates/tshirt-black.png` -- these need to be high-quality flat-lay photos that actually exist and cover all product categories and colors.\n\n2. **No front/back toggle**: Every competitor has this. Add two small buttons below the preview to switch between front and back views.\n\n3. **Print area is only visible when content exists**: Change to always-visible subtle dashed border. This gives users confidence about where their design will print even before they start typing.\n\n4. **The Design Studio and ProductPersonalizer are partially redundant**: DesignStudio wraps ProductPersonalizer in its Text tab, but ProductPersonalizer also has its own Dialog with image upload. These should be consolidated -- the DesignStudio should be the single entry point, with ProductPersonalizer extracted to be a pure form component (no Dialog wrapper).\n\n5. **No indication of print dimensions**: Users of Printful see the actual print size (e.g., \"12 x 16 inches\"). Adding a small label like \"Print area: 30 x 40 cm\" beneath the preview builds confidence.\n\n---\n\n### Sources\n\n- [Printful Design Maker](https://www.printful.com/design-maker)\n- [How Does
